image_type: str = IMAGE_JPEG):\n   215→    \"\"\"\n   216→    Evaluates if a predicate can be inferred from the content/image, judged by LLM\n   217→    \"\"\"\n   218→    if image_path is not None file exists in an ownCloud directory using WebDAV and download it if found.\n   461→\n   462→    Parameters:\n   463→        file_name (str): The name of the file to check and download.\n   464→        dir_name (str): The directory path on NextCloud.\n   465→        output_file_path (str): Path to save the downloaded file locally.\n   466→\n   467→    Returns:\n   468→        bool: True if the file was found and downloaded successfully, False otherwise.\n   469→    \"\"\"\n   470→    # Construct WebDAV URL for the directory\n   471→    WEBDAV_BASE_URL = f\"{OWNCLOUD_URL}/remote.php/webdav/\"\n   472→    server_url = f\"{WEBDAV_BASE_URL}{dir_name.strip('/')}/\"\n   473→    headers = {'Depth': '1'}  # Request depth for PROPFIND\n   474→\n   475→    try:\n   476→        # Check if the file exists in the directory\n   477→        response = requests.request(\n   478→            method=\"PROPFIND\",\n   479→            url=server_url,\n   480→            headers=headers,\n   481→            auth=HTTPBasicAuth(OWNCLOUD_USERNAME, OWNCLOUD_PASSWORD)\n   482→        )\n   483→\n   484→        if response.status_code == 207:\n   485→            # Parse the XML response to find the file\n   486→            root = ET.fromstring(response.text)\n   487→            for response_element in root.findall(\".//{DAV:}response\"):\n   488→                href = response_element.find(\"{DAV:}href\").text\n   489→                if file_name in href:\n   490→                    logging.info(f\"File '{file_name}' found. Proceeding to download.\")\n   491→                    \n   492→                    # Construct full file URL\n   493→                    file_url = server_url + file_name\n   494→\n   495→                    # Download the file\n   496→                    download_response = requests.get(\n   497→                        file_url,\n   498→                        auth=HTTPBasicAuth(OWNCLOUD_USERNAME, OWNCLOUD_PASSWORD),\n   499→                        stream=True\n   500→                    )\n   501→\n   502→                    if download_response.status_code == 200:\n   503→                        with open(output_file_path, \"wb\") as file:\n   504→                            for chunk in download_response.iter_content(chunk_size=8192):\n   505→                                file.write(chunk)\n   506→                        logging.info(f\"File '{file_name}' downloaded successfully to '{output_file_path}'.\")\n   507→                        return True\n   508→                    else:\n   509→                        logging.error(f\"Failed to download file '{file_name}'. HTTP Status: {download_response.status_code}\")\n   510→                        return False\n   511→\n   512→            # File not found in the directory\n   513→            logging.warning(f\"File '{file_name}' not found in directory '{dir_name}'.\")\n   514→            return False\n   515→\n   516→        else:\n   517→            # Log unexpected HTTP status codes\n   518→            logging.error(f\"Error checking directory. HTTP Status: {response.status_code}, Response: {response.text}\")\n   519→            return False\n   520→\n   521→    except requests.RequestException as e:\n   522→        logging.warning(f\"Failed to check or download file in ownCloud directory: {e}\")\n   523→        return False\n   524→\n   525→\n   526→def check_file_in_owncloud_directory(file_name, dir_name):\n   527→    \"\"\"\n   528→ check_and_download_file(file_name, dir_name, output_file_path):\n    \"\"\"\n    Check if a file exists in an ownCloud directory using WebDAV and download it if found.\n\n    Parameters:\n        file_name (str): The name of the file to check and download.\n        dir_name (str): The directory path on NextCloud.\n        output_file_path (str): Path to save the downloaded file locally.\n\n    Returns:\n        bool: True if the file was found and downloaded successfully, False otherwise.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Construct WebDAV URL for the directory\n    WEBDAV_BASE_URL = file exists in the directory\n        response = requests.request(\n            method=\"PROPFIND\",\n            url=server_url,\n            headers=headers,\n            auth=HTTPBasicAuth(OWNCLOUD_USERNAME, OWNCLOUD_PASSWORD)\n        )\n\n        if response.status_code == 207:\n            # Parse the XML response to find the file\n            root = HTTP Status: {download_response.status_code}\")\n                        return False\n\n            # File not found in the directory\n            logging.warning(f\"File '{file_name}' not found in directory '{dir_name}'.\")\n            return False\n\n        else:\n            # Log unexpected HTTP status codes\n            logging.error(f\"Error checking directory. HTTP Status: {response.status_code}, Response: {response.text}\")\n            return False\n\n    except requests.RequestException as e:\n        logging.warning(f\"Failed to check or download file
